# Saved scoring results
 This report presents the saved inferential and descriptive results . The main analysis uses the specified primary seed  20260919; News  Holm  family is fixed at  2, The notice family is fixed at  3. Negative  A−B  indicates  A  has lower error .

 The main news window is  214  days ,15,408  common keys ; The main notice window is  61  target days ,527,040  common keys . Comparisons lacking inputs or complete predictions retain their recorded status , the main window is fixed .

## confirmation-A1  Additional scoring inputs (input_definitions  Implementation ; The primary decision rule is fixed )

Binding decisions SHA-256 at implementation dca801989de8834709e93bb0f57934734411506546e13082bfd8df182dddead5; input_definitions SPEC SHA-256 e1622a6adb9a7506ab555444bc799343d65b5f086a7c859e7d7557daa4994a8c. confirmation/A1/DECISIONS_A1_source.md (binding decisions); confirmation/input_definitions/SPEC.md (implementation rules)

###  Statements by forecasting task ( Decision  9)

News task (specified Holm family of 2, read on its own): L_count−M: NO_RELIABLE_INCREMENT_DETECTED; L_chen−M: NO_RELIABLE_INCREMENT_DETECTED.

Notice task (specified Holm family of 3, read on its own): L14−M_notice: CONDITIONAL_IMPROVEMENT; L_Laz−M_notice: CONDITIONAL_IMPROVEMENT; L_chen_notice−M_notice: CONDITIONAL_IMPROVEMENT.

Each task is read and reported on its own. No sentence pools the news and notice families into a general claim about LLM-processed content; with two families each at alpha = .05, the chance that at least one rejects when both global nulls hold is up to 1 - 0.95^2 = 9.75% under independence (confirmation-A1 decision 9).

Window wording (decision 10): a retrospective out-of-sample confirmation for this study, part of whose window had been examined preceding elsewhere in the project (confirmation/A1/DATA_EXPOSURE_LOG.md).

W2_D (decision 4): COMPLETE.

NPM (decision 7): COMPLETE.

W_Bai is reported unavailable under confirmation-A1 decision 4, for two reasons: (1) the frozen selection returned BAI_UNAVAILABLE_TRAINING_PRICE_PREREQUISITE; the frozen code requires every day 2021-10-01..2024-12-31 to have 72 labels available before 2024-12-31T14:00Z, which 2024-12-31 can never meet (its last hourly label is available at 2025-01-01 00:05 AEST); (2) independently of that input_checks, the frozen thresholds (title >= 200, body >= 5,000) select a single candidate ("nsw") on the 1,349-article training corpus, and it fails the bilateral Granger filter (A1 results C5 and C5b). The frozen W_Bai code is unchanged; the specified W2_D arm uses Bai's stated vocabulary sizes instead.

LEAR convergence diagnostic (template rule; descriptive, no accuracy): state=DESCRIPTIVE_ONLY; daily jobs=214; hour fits=61632; coordinate-descent fits with a ConvergenceWarning=1; largest reported gap/tolerance=1.30394265233; hour fits with a LARS ConvergenceWarning=3295; LARS warnings by type={'REGRESSORS_IN_ACTIVE_SET_DEGENERATE': 14402, 'EARLY_STOPPING_LARS_PATH': 2222}.

Shift-support rule (decision 5), arms M_shift_notice, P_shift, NPP: Under confirmation-A1 decision 5 this contrast is evaluated only on the 38 shift-supported target days 2026-08-24..2026-09-30 with the sealed predictions (no refit); target days 2026-08-01..2026-08-23 are NOT_EVALUABLE_SHIFT_SUPPORT_GAP.

##  Scope of conclusions 

Historical conditional design; asymptotic covariance-stationarity DM assumptions; 135/61 days shorter than Lago test-year recommendation; nominal fixed-prediction intervals; no equivalence or mechanism identification.

Both bootstrap intervals are nominal per contrast, conditional on sealed fitted predictions and the specified resampling design; they are not simultaneous family intervals or unconditional training-uncertainty intervals. There is no specified equivalence margin or equivalence test. Region/horizon, coverage, clipping, severe-event and 2025 validation diagnostics do not replace the pooled main-seed primary decisions.
